Let's design final algorithm with phases:
A. Generate candidate sequences of various lengths.
B. Optimize each candidate using accelerated multiplicative descent (MMD) with exact score tracking.
C. Final startup and coordinate local search on best.

Detailed helper functions:
- `as_valid_array(x, n=None)`: clip 0..1000, finite, length. Return list maybe.
- `fast_convolve(x, y)`: use np.convolve maybe; if len large use FFT? Implement both. For x,y real. Need exact enough. Use direct for len product <= maybe 2e6, else FFT. But evaluate_sequence uses np.convolve direct; our scores may differ by rounding; okay. For internal many evals, use rfft to speed for n>1024. Implement `conv_full(a,b)`:
   la,lb. If la*lb <= 2_000_000? np.convolve. Else use rfft with next power of two >= la+lb-1. Ensure real. But rfft may introduce tiny negative; reflect.
- `score_norm(p)`: p normalized? We can normalize to sum 1 (if sum>0). c=conv_full(p,p); return 2*len(p)*max(c) (since sum=1), maybe exact list for evaluate if needed. If sum small invalid return inf. Need handle p all zeros.
- `score_sequence_list(a)`: for final, call evaluate_sequence? Internal can use. Use fast evaluate to avoid list conversions: We'll implement `exact_score_array(a)`: a clipped, sum, if sum<0.01 inf, return 2*n*max(conv)/sum^2. Since our normalized can return same. It uses conv_full.
- `normalize_positive(p)`: p = np.clip(p,0,1000); s=sum; if s==0 return None; return p/s.
- `softmax_weights(d, beta)`: m=len(d); shift d max; exp(beta*(d-dmax)); w sum. If sum 0? use ones.
- `gradient_h(p, beta)`: p normalized length m. c=conv_full(p,p); d=m*c. w=softmax_weights(d,beta); phi_d=np.sum(w*d); conv_wp=np.convolve(w,p,mode='valid')? Use FFT for w*p valid? np.convolve(w,p,mode='valid') returns length m (since w len 2m-1>=m). For m large, np.convolve with mode valid O(m^2) still; can use FFT? Since w length 2m-1, p length m, full conv length 3m-2, valid length m. For frequency, we can compute full = rfft(w,L)*rfft(p,L) with L>=3m-2, take valid slice? We only need h = 4*m*conv_wp. Could compute full and slice `valid` corresponds indices m-1? For np.convolve(w,p,'valid') with w longer: output length m (when w len 2m-1, p m): mode valid takes min len p, output[k] = ∑_{i=0}^{m-1} p_i w_{k+i}? Let's verify earlier: np.convolve(w,p,'valid') for w len3,p len2 gave [w0*a+w1*b, w1*a+w2*b]. This equals for k=0,1 w_{k+i} p_i. So output index j = sum_{i=0}^{m-1} w_{j+i} p_i, j=0..m-1. That is what we want. Can compute via cross-correlation: valid = cross-correlation of w with p. Use FFT: full = convolution(w,p) length 3m-2. Which slice corresponds? For w len Lw=2m-1, p len m. Full conv index t=∑ p_i w_{t-i}. Valid output j=∑_{i=0}^{m-1} w_{j+i}p_i. Set t = j+? w index j+i = t-i => t - i = j+i => t=j+2i, not constant. Not simple. Use FFT cross-correlation by zero-padding reversed p? Alternative: conv_wp_j = ∑_{i=0}^{m-1} p_i w_{j+i}. Define q = p, r = w. This is cross-correlation ∑ p_i r_{j+i}. We can compute by FFT: if we reverse p? Let p_rev[m-1-i]=p_i. Convolution? (p_rev * w) at index j + m-1? (p_rev*w)[t] =∑_{i=0}^{m-1} p_i w_{t-i}. Set t=j+m-1 => ∑ p_i w_{j+m-1-i}. Not w_{j+i}. import numpy as np
import time

def next_power_of_two(x): ...
def conv_full(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b); nout=la+lb-1
    if la*lb <= 2000000: return np.convolve(a,b)
    L=1
    while L<nout: L*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return out[:nout]
```
For real, direct threshold maybe 1e6. For m=2048, 4M > threshold so FFT; good.

But for w*p valid, we can use FFT correlation:
```
def conv_valid_front(w,p):
    # w length >= p length, returns length p: out[j]=sum_i w[j+i]*p[i]
    # use irfft(fft(w, L)*conj(fft(p,L))) out[:len(p)]
    L=1
    while L < len(w)+len(p)-1: L*=2
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:len(p)]
```
Test with small: w=[1,2,3],p=[4,5], L=8. fft product inv -> correlation? At index0=1*4+2*5=14, index1=2*4+3*5=23. Good. Our desired for gradient: conv(w,p,'valid') = same. Great.

```
def score_norm_array(p):
    p=np.clip(np.asarray(p,float),0,1000)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    if s != 1: p=p/s
    c=conv_full(p,p)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return 2.0*len(p)*float(np.max(c))
```

def mmd_step(p, beta, alphas):
    h=...; bestp=p; bestscore=score_norm_array(p)
    for a in alphas:
       p_try=p*np.exp(-a*h)
       p_try=np.clip(p_try,0,1000)
       s=p_try.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       p_try/=s
       score=score_norm_array(p_try)
       if score < bestscore - 1e-12:
           return p_try, score, True
    return bestp,bestscore,False
```

Let's design `run_mmd(p0, time_budget, beta0, beta1, label, verbose)`:
```
def run_mmd(p0, time_budget, beta0=10, beta1=150, patience=150, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+time_budget
    p=normalize_positive(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    m=len(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_norm_array(p)
    alphas=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]
    beta=beta0
    iterations=0
    stagnant=0
    last_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        # compute h
        c=conv_full(p,p); d=m*c; ... w, phi_d
        h=conv_valid_front(w,p); maxh=np.max(h)
        if not finite or maxh==0: break
        h=h/maxh
        base_score=score_norm_array(p) # maybe current exact
        improved=False
        for a in alphas:
            p_try=p*np.exp(-a*h)
            s=p_try.sum()
            if s<=0: continue
            p_try=p_try/s
            if p_try is finite:
                score_try=score_norm_array(p_try)
                if score_try < base_score - 1e-12:
                    p=p_try; base_score=score_try; improved=True; break
        if improved:
            if base_score < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=base_score; last_improve=iterations; print
            stagnant=0
        else:
            stagnant +=1
            # Try random perturbation if stagnant > patience/3
            if stagnant > patience//2:
                # random log perturbation mild
                noise = rng? Need pass rng. Use global rng? Create np.random.default_rng seed.
                p = normalize_positive(p*np.exp(noise*0.1))
                # maybe reset beta lower
                beta=max(beta0,beta*0.5)
        iterations+=1
        if iterations % 50 ==0:
            beta *= 1.1
            if beta>beta1: beta=beta1
        if stagnant > patience:
            # bigger perturbation
            p = normalize_positive(p*np.exp(noise*0.5))
            beta=max(beta0,beta*0.5)
            stagnant=0
```
Need rng top-level or pass. Use global `RNG = np.random.default_rng(123)`? But top-level mutable okay. Or create local `rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)` not closure.

```
def resample_sequence(a, m):
    # returns length m normalized positive array via linear interpolation with half sample endpoints
    a = valid normalized; n=len(a)
    if m==n return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0,1,n) maybe use indices: (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    x_new = (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    vals=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    vals=np.maximum(vals,0)
    s=vals.sum(); if s>0 vals/=s
    return vals
```

Function `run_candidate_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta0, beta1, verbose=False)`:
Implementation details:
```
def run_candidate_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta0=8.0, beta1=250.0, verbose=False):
    p = normalize_positive(p0)
    if p is None: return None, np.inf
    m=len(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_norm_array(p)
    current_score=best_score
    beta=beta0
    max_iterations? no
    alphas = np.array([0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0])
    stall=0; stagn_limit=max(50, int(0.03*?)) maybe 100
    last_score=best_score
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        # compute gradient
        c=conv_full(p,p)
        d=m*c
        shift=np.max(d)
        expv=np.exp(beta*(d-shift))
        wsum=expv.sum()
        if not finite or wsum==0: weights uniform
        else w=expv/wsum
        h=conv_valid_front(w,p)
        if not finite: perturb
        maxh=np.max(h)
        if maxh<=1e-300: h_zero
        else h=h/maxh
        base=current_score # exact current
        chosen=None; chosen_score=base
        # maybe evaluate smooth current phi? not needed
        for a in alphas:
            dec = np.exp(-a*h)
            p_try = p*dec
            s=p_try.sum()
            if s<=0: continue
            p_try = p_try/s
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_try)): continue
            sc = score_norm_array(p_try)
            # accept if improves base or best by tiny? use base
            if sc < chosen_score - 1e-12:
                chosen=p_try; chosen_score=sc
        if chosen is not None:
            p=chosen; current_score=chosen_score
            if current_score < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score
                if verbose: print
            stall=0
        else:
            # try smaller alphas? generate alpha = 0.01*rng
            # if no improvement, perturb
            stall +=1
            if stall > 30:
                # random multiplicative noise
                noise=rng.normal(0,0.05,len(p))
                p=r.normalize_positive(p*np.exp(noise))
                current_score=score_norm_array(p)
                beta=max(beta0,beta*0.7)
                stall=0
                # if current score much worse than best, reset to best
                if current_score > best_score*1.02:
                    p=best.copy(); current_score=best_score
        # anneal beta every 20 iterations
        iteration_count +=1
        if iteration_count%20==0:
            beta *= 1.1
            if beta>beta1: beta=beta1
        if stall > 200:
            # bigger reset
            p=best.copy(); current_score=best_score
            beta=max(beta0,beta*0.5)
            stall=0
    return best,best_score
```

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time

def _next_pow2(x):
    L=1
    while L<x: L <<=1
    return L

def _conv_full(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b); nout=la+lb-1
    if nout<=0: return np.array([])
    if la*lb <= 1000000:
        return np.convolve(a,b).astype(np.float64)
    L=_next_pow2(nout)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, L)
    return out[:nout]

def _conv_valid_front(w,p):
    lenw=len(w); lenp=len(p); nout=lenp
    L=_next_pow2(lenw+lenp-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:nout]

def _normalize(p, floor=0.0):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
   if p.ndim!=1 or p.size==0: return None
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0)
   p=np.clip(p, 0, 1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: return None
   return p/s

def _exact_score(p):
   p=_normalize(p)
   if p is None: return np.inf
   m=len(p)
   c=_conv_full(p,p)
   return float(2.0*m*np.max(c))

def _soft_weights(d,beta):
   if beta <= 1e-8:
       return np.full(d.shape, 1.0/d.size)
   shift=np.max(d)
   e=np.exp(beta*(d-shift))
   s=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.full(d.shape,1/d.size)
   return e/s

def _smooth_phi(p,beta):
   m=len(p); c=_conv_full(p,p); d=m*c; w=_soft_weights(d,beta); return float(np.dot(w,d))

def _smooth_exact(p,beta):
   m=len(p); c=_conv_full(p,p); d=m*c; exact=float(2.0*np.max(d)); w=_soft_weights(d,beta); smooth=float(np.dot(w,d)); return exact, smooth

def _resample(a,m): ...

def _initial(m,rng,mode=None): ...

def _local_block_search(p, rng, time_limit, seed, verbose): ...

def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta0, beta1, verbose): ...

def propose_candidate(...):
    deadline=monotonic+budget-10
    parent = globals().get('height_sequence_1', [])
    parent_arr = _normalize(np.asarray(parent,float)) if parent else None
    # quick small searches
    top_candidates=[] (arr, score)
    lengths = [512,1024,2048] maybe small.
    for n in [512]:
       for i in range(2): start random; time per 1s; run_mmd; add
    # final length choose 4096, max by time? If budget large use 4096. final_n=4096 if budget >1200 else 2048
    starts=[]
    if parent_arr: starts.append(resample(parent_arr, final_n))
    if top_candidates: add resample(best small)
    add several random initial length final_n (asymmetric etc.)
    # allocate time: maybe 4 starts * (budget*0.2) each? Let optimize top starts shorter? Use list and iterate round-robin?
```

Algorithm `_run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=8, beta_end=250)`:
```
p=_normalize(p0)
if p is None: return None,inf
m=len(p)

alpha=0.1
stall=0; iterations=0
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
    # compute gradient from current p
    c=_conv_full(p,p); d=m*c
    w=_soft_weights(d,beta)
    h=_conv_valid_front(w,p)
    maxh=np.max(h)
    if not finite or maxh<=1e-12: break
    h=h/maxh
    # maybe current smooth from last update; recompute if not?
    # current_smooth already from _smooth_exact current? if p changed? yes.
    # Candidate alphas dynamic: centered on alpha
    alphas = [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,0.05*rng?]. unique
    current_best_candidate=None; best_key=(current_smooth, current_exact)
    for a in alphas:

       s=p_try.sum(); if s<=0: continue

       # key: primarily smooth, second exact

           best_key=(smooth_try, exact_try); candidate=p_try; ...

Local block search after MMD:
```
def local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit, seed):
   p=normalize; best=p; best_score=exact.
   deadline=...
   stagnant=0
   while time:
      n=len(p)
      # choose width
      mode=rng.random()
      if mode<0.3: width=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,n//200)))
      elif <0.8: width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//100), max(3,n//20)))
      else: width=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//20), max(4,n//4)))
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
      # try factor range: choose delta from lognormal
      delta_abs = max(0.02, min(2.0, rng.normal(0,0.3)))
      sign=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
      cand=p.copy(); cand[start:start+width]*=exp(sign*delta_abs)
      cand=normalize(cand)
      sc=exact(cand)
      if sc<best_score: update; stagnant=0
      else: stagnant++
      if stagnant>100:
         # add noise to p
         p=normalize(p*exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))
         best? keep
         stagnant=0
      if p is not best? If degraded, maybe reset? keep p for random? Use p as current, but best tracked. If p exact > best*1.02, reset p=best.
   return best,best_score
```
Also try multiple factors per block: Instead of one random, choose block and evaluate factors [0.5,0.8,1.25,1.6] and choose best. This is more efficient. Each block eval conv FFT maybe. Could sample 5 blocks per iteration. But MMD maybe enough.

- For each iteration choose width as above, then for f in [0.5,0.75,0.9,1/0.9,1.25,1.5,2] evaluate candidate and choose best (including factor1). Accept if best factor !=1 and score better. Also try negative via 1/f? Included. Use normalized exact. Track global. This deterministic hill-climb on block scales.
- To avoid too many convs: 5 factors * maybe 100 iterations * time. Fine.

```

if mode==0:
   # parent-like feedback? random smooth: z=1+rng.normal; p=softplus(z*0.5+sum sinusoid)
if mode==1:
   a=float(rng.uniform(0.05,3)); b=...; p=t**a*(1-t)**b; p = p+1e-6
elif mode==2:
   K=1+rng.integers(0,3); p=zeros; for each choose weight positive, center sorted, sigma; p += weight*exp(-((t-c)/sigma)^2)
elif mode==3:
   K=rng.integers(3,8); knots=sort; heights=rng.lognormal; p=interp; p=p+1e-6
elif mode==4:
   z=1 + sum_{k=1}^{12} amp[k]*cos(2π k t + phase); amp maybe exponential; p=np.log1p(np.exp(z))
elif mode==5:
   # asymmetric power with random edge zeros:
   a,b, p = (t+eps)**a*((1-t)+eps)**b maybe; but if a<1 high edge.
# normalize; add tiny uniform to avoid all zero.
```

Now, local block search implementation in detail:
```
def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit):
    p=_normalize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=len(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,time_limit)
    stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        # choose width
        x=rng.random()
        if x<0.25:
           w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//200+1)))
        elif x<0.75:
           w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//40)))
        else:
           w=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//40), max(4,min(n, n//5+1))))
        if w<1: w=1
        if w>=n: w=max(1,n//2)
        start=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
        base=p.copy()
        # Try a couple factors; maybe choose 4 random plus extremes
        factors = np.array([0.5,0.8,1.25,1.6,0.625,1.4])
        # Add random factor
        factors = np.concatenate((factors, np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,3))))
        best_local_score=best_score
        best_local_factor=1.0
        for f in factors:
            cand=base.copy()
            cand[start:start+w] *= float(f)
            cand=_normalize(cand)
            if cand is None: continue
            sc=_exact_score(cand)
            if sc < best_local_score - 1e-12:
                best_local_score=sc; best_local_factor=f; best_local=cand
        # after loop if improvement, update p and best? p current maybe not. Set p=best_local copy.
        if best_local_factor !=1.0:
            p=best_local.copy()
            best=p.copy(); best_score=best_local_score
            stagnant=0
        else:
            stagnant +=1
        if stagnant > 80:
            p=_normalize(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))
            if p is None: p=best.copy()
            stagnant=0
            if _exact_score(p) > best_score*1.05:
                p=best.copy()
    return best,best_score
```
Could be many exact_score calls: factors ~9, each FFT conv. For n=4096, factor eval maybe 0.0005s? 9* maybe 0.005, 200 iterations/s? Good.

But `best_local` variable may be referenced before assignment if no factor improves. Initialize `best_local=None`. Good.

Maybe can improve by accepting factor if local score < current exact `current_score`, not global best, to climb. But global best safer. We can maintain current `p` and current_score; if block factor improves current but not best, accept. Then if current degrades, reset. Let's implement simple: track current_score=p exact. For each factor, if sc < current_score improve current. Choose best factor > maybe accept even if worsens global? But local search for final should mostly improve. Use current_score.

```
current_score=_exact_score(p)
for f: candidate; if sc<current_score: current=cand; current_score=sc
if current changed: p=current
if current_score<best_score: update
```

```
def _mmd_metrics_and_gradient(p, beta):
    m=len(p)
    c=_conv_full(p,p)
    d=m*c
    w=_soft_weights(d,beta)
    h=_conv_valid_front(w,p)
    h=np.clip(h,0,None)
    maxh=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    smooth=float(np.dot(w,d))
    exact=float(2.0*np.max(d))
    return exact, smooth, h, maxh
```

Pseudo full for `_run_mmd`:
```
def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=300.0, verbose=False):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0: ...
    m=len(p)
    best = p.copy(); best_exact = _exact_score(p)
    deadline=...
    beta=max(1.0,float(beta_start))
    alpha=0.1
    stall=0; it=0
    # compute current exact/smooth
    exact, smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
    while time < deadline:
        exact, smooth, h, maxh = _gradient_metrics(p, beta)
        if maxh <= 1e-15:
            # no gradient; perturb
        else:
            h = h/maxh
            tests=[]
            for mult in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
                a=alpha*mult
                if 1e-5 <= a <= 20: tests.append(a)
            # remove duplicates approximate
            best_trial=None; best_smooth=smooth; best_exact=exact
            for a in tests:
                 dec=np.exp(-a*h) # h max1, a up to maybe 4*alpha. If alpha 1, a=4 dec~0.018.
                 p_try=p*dec
                 s=p_try.sum()
                 if s <=0: continue
                 p_try=p_try/s
                 if not finite: continue
                 e,s2=_exact_smooth(p_try,beta)
                 if s2 < best_smooth - 1e-12:
                     best_trial=p_try; best_smooth=s2; best_exact=e; best_a=a
            if best_trial is not None:
                 p=best_trial; current_e=best_exact; current_smooth=best_smooth; alpha=best_a; stall=0
                 if current_e < best_exact:
                     best=p.copy(); best_exact=current_e; verbose print
                 # if degraded too much relative best and random: reset later
            else:
                 stall +=1
                 alpha=max(0.001,alpha*0.6)
                 if stall > 15:
                     noise_std = 0.05 + 0.001*stall
                     p_try=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,min(0.5,noise_std),m))
                     p_try=_normalize(p_try)
                     if p_try is not None:
                         p=p_try
                         exact, smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)
                         if exact <= best_exact*1.02:
                             # keep
                         else:
                             p=best.copy(); exact,smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)
                     stall=0
                 if stall > 50:
                     p=best.copy(); exact,smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta); alpha=0.1; stall=0; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.5)
        it +=1

Let's define:
```
def _gradient_metrics(p, beta):
    m=len(p)
    c=_conv_full(p,p)
    d=m*c
    w=_soft_weights(d,beta)
    h=_conv_valid_front(w,p)
    h=np.where(h>0,h,0.0)
    maxh=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    smooth=float(np.dot(w,d))
    exact=float(2.0*np.max(d))
    return exact, smooth, h, maxh
```

Now, ensure `np.interp` for resample handles length. In `_resample`:
```
def _resample(arr, m):
    a=_normalize(arr)
    if a is None: return None
    n=a.size
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    # add tiny to zeros
    out += 1e-12
    return _normalize(out)
```
Adding 1e-12 may not matter. For m 8192, interpolation fine.

`_initial_sequence` with t half-sample avoids exact zero endpoints; but we may want zeros at edges? Gaps can create zeros via clipping; but adding tiny floor. Use small floor 1e-12 to allow positive. Normalization sum1.

Implement `_initial_sequence`:
```
def _initial_sequence(m, rng, mode=None):
    t=(np.arange(m, dtype=float)+0.5)/m
    if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    p=np.ones(m)
    if mode==0:
        # sinusoid softplus
        z=1.0
        for k in range(1,13):
            amp=float(rng.normal(0, 1.0/(k+1)))
            ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
            z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t+ph)
        p=_softplus(z)
    elif mode==1:
        a=float(rng.uniform(0.05,3.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(0.05,3.0))
        p=np.power(np.clip(t,1e-8,1-1e-8), a)*np.power(np.clip(1-t,1e-8,1-1e-8), b)
    elif mode==2:
        K=int(rng.integers(1,4)); p=np.zeros(m)
        centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K))
        sigmas=rng.uniform(0.02,0.35,K)
        weights=rng.uniform(0.5,2.0,K)
        for c,s,w in zip(centers,sigmas,weights):
            p += w*np.exp(-((t-c)/(s+1e-6))**2)
    elif mode==3:
        K=int(rng.integers(3,9))
        knots=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K))
        heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K)
        p=np.interp(t, knots, heights)
    elif mode==4:
        # piecewise constant random blocks
        K=int(rng.integers(3,16))
        edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1))
        blocks=[0]+list(edges)+[1]
        heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K)
        for i in range(K):
             p[int(round(blocks[i]*m)):max(int(round(blocks[i+1]*m)),...)] = heights[i]
    else:
        # logistic mix / edge bump
        c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); k=float(rng.uniform(5,30))
        p=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-k*(t-c)))
        p *= np.exp(-((t-c)/0.3)**2)? maybe
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.ones(m)
    p += 1e-12
    return p/p.sum()
```
Add 1e-12 changes sum negligible. For mode4 blank if K=1 edges list? K>=3 so K-1 edges. fine.

Need no lambda; in mode4 use loops.

Parent start: use `_resample`. Add noise to break symmetry: in build starts, after resample, `p0 = _normalize(p0 * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))`.

Let's write code in analysis to review.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x):
    L=1
    while L < int(x):
        L <<= 1
    return L

def _conv_full(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b)
    if la==0 or lb==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    nout=la+lb-1
    if la*lb <= 2000000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    L=_next_pow2(nout)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, L)
    return out[:nout].astype(float, copy=False)

def _conv_valid_front(w,p):
    lenw=len(w); lenp=len(p)
    if lenp==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    # w should be at least p length. L >= lenw.
    L=_next_pow2(max(lenw, 2*lenp))
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:lenp].astype(float, copy=False)
```
Check L: need L >= lenw = 2lenp-1. `_next_pow2(max(lenw,2*lenp))` picks 2lenp. Good. If lenp=1, lenw? In MMD w length 1? m=1; L=2. okay.

```
def _sanitize_vec(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
   if p.ndim!=1 or p.size==0: return None
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
   p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
   return p/s
```

`_exact_score`:
```
def _exact_score(p):
   p=_sanitize_vec(p)
   if p is None: return float('inf')
   m=p.size
   c=_conv_full(p,p)
   if c.size==0: return inf
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
   return float(2.0*m*np.max(c))
```

`_soft_weights`:
```
def _soft_weights(d,beta):
   d=np.asarray(d,dtype=float)
   n=d.size
   if n==0: return np.array([],float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): d=np.where(np.isfinite(d),d,0.0)
   if beta <= 1e-8:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n,float)
   shift=float(np.max(d))
   if not np.isfinite(shift): return np.full(n,1.0/n,float)
   e=np.exp(beta*(d-shift))
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(n,1.0/n,float)
   return e/s
```

`_exact_smooth`:
```
def _exact_smooth(p,beta):
   p=_sanitize_vec(p)
   if p is None: return inf,inf
   m=p.size
   c=_conv_full(p,p)
   if c.size==0: return inf,inf
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
   d=m*c
   exact=float(2.0*max(0.0,np.max(d))) if d.size else inf
   w=_soft_weights(d,beta)
   smooth=float(np.dot(w,d))
   if not np.isfinite(smooth): smooth=exact
   return exact, smooth
```

`_gradient_metrics`:
```
def _gradient_metrics(p,beta):
   p=_sanitize_vec(p)
   if p is None: return inf,inf,None,0.0
   m=p.size
   c=_conv_full(p,p)
   d=m*c
   if not finite c ...
   w=_soft_weights(d,beta)
   h=_conv_valid_front(w,p)
   if h.size==0: h=np.array([0.0])
   if not finite h: h=where
   h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
   maxh=float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
   smooth=float(np.dot(w,d))
   exact=float(2.0*max(0.0,np.max(d)))
   return exact,smooth,h,maxh
```

`_initial_sequence` as above. Need `_softplus` top-level? Use inline in mode0: `p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50)))`. If z negative large, exp ~0, log1p~0, p zero. Then normalize could fail if all zeros; add 1e-8.

```
def _initial_sequence(m, rng, mode=None):
    t=(np.arange(m,float)+0.5)/float(m)
    if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    p=np.ones(m,float)
    if mode == 0:
       z=1.0
       for k in range(1,13):
          amp=float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0/(k+1)))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2*np.pi))
          z = z + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
       p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 50)))
    elif mode == 1:
       a=float(rng.uniform(0.05,3.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(0.05,3.0))
       tt=np.clip(t,1e-8,1-1e-8)
       p=np.power(tt,a)*np.power(1.0-tt,b)
    elif mode == 2:
       K=int(rng.integers(1,4)); p=np.zeros(m,float)
       centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K))
       sigmas=rng.uniform(0.02,0.35,K)
       weights=rng.uniform(0.5,2.0,K)
       for ii in range(K):
          p = p + float(weights[ii]) * np.exp(-((t - float(centers[ii])) / max(1e-6,float(sigmas[ii]))) ** 2)
    elif mode == 3:
       K=int(rng.integers(3,9))
       knots=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K))
       heights=rng.lognormal(0.0,1.0,K)
       p=np.interp(t,knots,heights)
    elif mode == 4:
       K=int(rng.integers(3,16))
       edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K-1))
       blocks=[0.0] += list? can't use += with list? Use `blocks=[0.0]; blocks.extend(list(edges)); blocks.append(1.0)`. 
       heights=rng.lognormal(0.0,1.0,K)
       p=np.zeros(m,float)
       for ii in range(K):
          s=max(0,int(round(blocks[ii]*m)))
          e=max(s+1,int(round(blocks[ii+1]*m)))
          p[s:e]=float(heights[ii])
    else:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); k=float(rng.uniform(5.0,30.0))
       p=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(k*(t-c), -50, 50)))
       p *= np.exp(-((t-c)/0.25)**2)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    p=p+1e-10
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: p=np.ones(m)
    else: p=p/s
    return p
```
`blocks=[0.0]; blocks.extend(list(edges));` works.

`_two_block_best`:
```
def _two_block_best(m):
   best=None; best_score=inf
   if m<4: return None
   for a in [0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85]:
      idx=int(round(a*m))
      if idx<=0 or idx>=m: continue
      for ratio in [0.3,0.6,1.0,1.7,2.8,5.0]:
         p=np.ones(m,float)
         p[idx:]=float(ratio)
         p=_sanitize_vec(p)
         if p is None: continue
         sc=_exact_score(p)
         if sc<best_score:
            best_score=sc; best=p.copy()
   return best
```
This assumes first block height 1, second ratio. It doesn't test reversed ratio? a values from 0.25 to0.85, ratios can be high/low; likely covers symmetric. Also maybe optimal no-gap? yes adjacent. We could also test a gap? not.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(arr, m):
   a=_sanitize_vec(arr)
   if a is None: return None
   n=a.size
   if n==m: return a.copy()
   if n==1:
      return np.full(m, float(a[0])/m? wait if a sum1, a[0]=1. resample constant ones? return ones/m? But p normalized: np.full(n?); if n=1, input [1], output ones (sum1).
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   out=out+1e-12
   return _sanitize_vec(out)
```
If n=1 and a[0]=1, output should be ones(m) not ones/m? Normalized sum1 means entries 1/m. Wait if input length1 nonnegative normalized sum1 => [1]. Resampling to length m should be constant probability density: each entry 1/m, which is np.ones(m) before normalization. Normalize gives 1/m. So `return np.ones(m,float)`.

def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit):
    p=_sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <=0: return None,inf
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(p)
    cur_score=best_score
    deadline=...
    stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       n=len(p)
       if n <=1: break
       r=rng.random()
       if r < 0.3:
          w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//200 + 1)))
       elif r < 0.75:
          w=int(rng.integers(max(2, n//200), max(3, n//40 + 1)))
       else:
          low=max(3, n//40); high=max(low+1, min(n, n//5 + 1))
          w=int(rng.integers(low, high+1))
       w=max(1,min(n,w))
       start=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
       base=p.copy()
       current_best=base
       current_sc=cur_score
       factors=[0.4,0.7,0.9,1.1,1.3,1.7,2.5]
       # add random sign? use 1/factors included by list? 0.9/1.1.
       for f in factors:
          cand=base.copy()
          cand[start:start+w] *= float(f)
          cand=_sanitize_vec(cand)
          if cand is None: continue
          sc=_exact_score(cand)
          if sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
             current_sc=sc; current_best=cand
       if current_best is not base:
          p=current_best.copy()
          cur_score=current_sc
          if cur_score < best_score:
             best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
          stagnant=0
       else:
          stagnant +=1
          if stagnant > 60:
             p_try=_sanitize_vec(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.05,n)))
             if p_try is not None:
                sc=_exact_score(p_try)
                if sc <= best_score*1.05:
                   p=p_try; cur_score=sc
                else:
                   p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score
             stagnant=0
       if stagnant > 300:
          p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score; stagnant=0
    return best,best_score
```

`_run_mmd` final. Need a `verbose` param; if verbose and best improves print. No closures.

```
def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=1000.0, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    if time_limit <=0: return p, _exact_score(p)
    m=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_exact=_exact_score(p)
    cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta_start)
    beta=float(beta_start)
    if beta <=0: beta=1.
    alpha=0.1
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    stall=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       cur_exact, cur_smooth, h, maxh = _gradient_metrics(p, beta)
       if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-15:
          # perturb
          p_try=_sanitize_vec(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.05,m)))
          if p_try is not None:
             e,s2=_exact_smooth(p_try,beta)
             if e <= best_exact*1.05:
                p=p_try; cur_exact=e; cur_smooth=s2
          stall +=1
       else:
          h=np.asarray(h,float)
          h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
          h=h/maxh
          tests=[]
          for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
             a=alpha*mult
             if a >= 1e-4 and a <= 20.0:
                tests.append(float(a))
          if len(tests)==0:
             tests=[0.05]
          chosen=None; ch_smooth=cur_smooth; ch_exact=cur_exact; ch_a=alpha
          for a in tests:
             dec=np.exp(-a*h)
             p_try=p*dec
             s=float(p_try.sum())
             if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): continue
             p_try=p_try/s
             if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_try)): continue
             e,s2=_exact_smooth(p_try,beta)
             if not np.isfinite(s2): continue
             if s2 + 1e-11*max(1.0, ch_smooth) < ch_smooth:
                chosen=p_try; ch_smooth=s2; ch_exact=e; ch_a=a
          if chosen is not None:
             p=chosen; cur_smooth=ch_smooth; cur_exact=ch_exact; alpha=ch_a; stall=0
             if cur_exact < best_exact:
                best=p.copy(); best_exact=cur_exact
                if verbose:
                   print(f"mmd n={m} iter={it} best={best_exact:.6f}")
             if cur_exact > best_exact*1.2 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                p=best.copy()
                cur_exact=best_exact
                cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)
          else:
             stall +=1
             alpha=max(1e-4, alpha*0.6)
             if stall >= 12:
                std = 0.05 if stall < 40 else min(0.4, 0.05 + 0.005*stall)
                p_try=_sanitize_vec(p*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, m)))
                if p_try is not None:
                   e,s2=_exact_smooth(p_try,beta)
                   if e <= best_exact*1.05:
                      p=p_try; cur_exact=e; cur_smooth=s2
                   else:
                      p=best.copy(); cur_exact=best_exact; cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)
                stall=0
             if stall > 80:
                p=best.copy(); cur_exact=best_exact; cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta); alpha=0.1; stall=0; beta=max(float(beta_start), beta*0.5)
       it += 1
       if it % 20 == 0:
          beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.15)
    return best, best_exact
```
Some repeated `cur_exact=best_exact; cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)` weird; just assign after recompute. Fix.

If cur_exact becomes inf due p invalid? Should not. If `best_exact` maybe inf? p normalized. good.

In candidate selection, if chosen candidate has exact `e` inf but smooth finite? We require `np.isfinite(s2)` but not e. Use if `not np.isfinite(e) or not np.isfinite(s2): continue`.

```
def _smooth_array(p, samples=2.0):
   p=_sanitize_vec(p)
   if p is None: return None
   s=float(samples)
   if s<=0 or p.size<5: return p
   rad=int(max(1, np.ceil(3*s)))
   k=np.arange(-rad,rad+1)
   kern=np.exp(-0.5*(k/s)**2)
   kern/=kern.sum()
   out=np.convolve(p, kern, mode='same')
   return _sanitize_vec(out)
```
This smoothing with 'same' can change length same. Use for two-block and parent starts with samples=0.5 or 1? Hard edges may remain but reduced. However, smoothing may help continuous. Apply to all final starts? If a start is random smooth, no harm. But if length 4096, kernel rad 3, good. Use samples=1.0. It may reduce score sometimes. sp=...; sp=_smooth_array(sp,0.5)
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)**2

using a multiplicative smoothed-max descent on the probability simplex,
random smooth/blocked initializations, deterministic two-block seeds,
coordinate block-scaling local search, and occasional up-polishing.

The final sequence is returned normalized to sum 1, which does not change
the score.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x."""
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _conv_full(a, b):
    """Full real convolution, using FFT for larger inputs."""
    la = len(a)
    lb = len(b)
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    nout = la + lb - 1
    if la * lb <= 2000000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, b), dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(nout)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, L)
    return out[:nout].astype(float, copy=False)


def _conv_valid_front(w, p):
    """
    Return v[j] = sum_i p[i] * w[j+i], j=0..len(p)-1.

    This is the 'valid' correlation needed for gradients of
    sum_j w_j * (p*p)[j].
    """
    lenw = len(w)
    lenp = len(p)
    if lenp == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    # Need circular length at least lenw.
    L = _next_pow2(max(lenw, 2 * lenp))
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:lenp].astype(float, copy=False)


def _sanitize_vec(p):
    """Clip finite non-negative vector and normalize to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return p / s


def _exact_score(p):
    """Internal exact score for a normalized vector."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    m = p.size
    c = _conv_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * m * np.max(c))


def _soft_weights(d, beta):
    """Softmax weights used for a differentiable max."""
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    n = d.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    shift = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(shift):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    e = np.exp(beta * (d - shift))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    return e / s


def _exact_smooth(p, beta):
    """Return (exact_score, smooth_max_d) for normalized p."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    m = p.size
    c = _conv_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    d = m * c
    exact = float(2.0 * max(0.0, np.max(d))) if d.size else float("inf")
    w = _soft_weights(d, beta)
    smooth = float(np.dot(w, d))
    if not np.isfinite(smooth):
        smooth = exact
    return exact, smooth


def _gradient_metrics(p, beta):
    """Exact score, smooth d-max, gradient h, and max(h)."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), None, 0.0
    m = p.size
    c = _conv_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), np.array([0.0]), 0.0
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    d = m * c
    exact = float(2.0 * max(0.0, np.max(d)))
    w = _soft_weights(d, beta)
    h = _conv_valid_front(w, p)
    if h.size == 0:
        h = np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    maxh = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    smooth = float(np.dot(w, d))
    return exact, smooth, h, maxh


def _smooth_array(p, samples=1.0):
    """Mild positive Gaussian smoothing, keeping sum 1."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    s = float(samples)
    if s <= 0.0 or p.size < 5:
        return p
    rad = int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0 * s)))
    k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1, dtype=float)
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / s) ** 2)
    kern /= kern.sum()
    out = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    return _sanitize_vec(out)


def _initial_sequence(m, rng, mode=None):
    """Create a diverse non-negative normalized start."""
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    t = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    if mode is None:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    p = np.ones(m, dtype=float)

    if mode == 0:
        # Soft-plus of a random low-degree cosine series.
        z = 1.0
        for k in range(1, 13):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / (k + 1)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z = z + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)))

    elif mode == 1:
        # Asymmetric beta-like density.
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 3.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 3.0))
        tt = np.clip(t, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        p = np.power(tt, a) * np.power(1.0 - tt, b)

    elif mode == 2:
        # Sum of random Gaussian bumps.
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        sigmas = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.35, K)
        weights = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0, K)
        for ii in range(K):
            c = float(centers[ii])
            s = max(1e-6, float(sigmas[ii]))
            w = float(weights[ii])
            p = p + w * np.exp(-((t - c) / s) ** 2)

    elif mode == 3:
        # Random piecewise-linear spline.
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        knots = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.interp(t, knots, heights)

    elif mode == 4:
        # Random block level function.
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 16))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1))
        blocks = [0.0]
        blocks.extend(list(edges))
        blocks.append(1.0)
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        for ii in range(K):
            s = max(0, int(round(blocks[ii] * m)))
            e = max(s + 1, int(round(blocks[ii + 1] * m)))
            e = min(m, e)
            p[s:e] = float(heights[ii])

    else:
        # Smooth asymmetric pulse / logistic hybrid.
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        k = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 30.0))
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(k * (t - c), -50.0, 50.0)))
        p = p * np.exp(-((t - c) / 0.25) ** 2)

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    p = p + 1e-10
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(m, dtype=float)
        s = float(p.sum())
    return p / s


def _two_block_best(m):
    """
    Quick deterministic 2-block(seed) by grid search.
    This gives an asymmetric low-score starting basin.
    """
    if m <= 4:
        return None
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for a in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85):
        idx = int(round(a * m))
        if idx <= 0 or idx >= m:
            continue
        for ratio in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.7, 2.8, 5.0):
            p = np.ones(m, dtype=float)
            p[idx:] = float(ratio)
            p = _sanitize_vec(p)
            if p is None:
                continue
            sc = _exact_score(p)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best = p.copy()
    return best


def _resample(arr, m):
    """Resample a normalized vector to length m by linear interpolation."""
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = out + 1e-12
    return _sanitize_vec(out)


def _cand_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _scored_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _prune_candidates(cands, keep):
    if len(cands) <= keep:
        return cands
    cands.sort(key=_cand_score_key)
    cands[:] = cands[:keep]
    return cands


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, score, keep=12):
    a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _exact_score(a)
    cands.append((float(score), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    _prune_candidates(cands, keep)


def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=1000.0, verbose=False):
    """
    Multiplicative descent on the probability simplex for a smoothed
    max convolution coefficient.  The exact score is tracked separately.
    """
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return p, _exact_score(p)

    m = int(p.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / float(m)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_exact = _exact_score(p)
    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta_start)

    beta = float(max(1.0, beta_start))
    beta_max = float(max(beta, beta_end))
    alpha = 0.1
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -10000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cur_exact, cur_smooth, h, maxh = _gradient_metrics(p, beta)

        if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-15:
            # Degenerate direction: perturb.
            p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, m)))
            if p_try is not None:
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                if np.isfinite(e) and e <= best_exact * 1.05:
                    p = p_try
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = e, s2
            stall += 1
        else:
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            h = h / maxh

            tests = []
            for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                a = alpha * mult
                if 1e-4 <= a <= 20.0:
                    tests.append(float(a))
            if not tests:
                tests = [1e-4, 0.05]

            chosen = None
            ch_smooth = cur_smooth
            ch_exact = cur_exact
            ch_a = alpha

            for a in tests:
                dec = np.exp(-a * h)
                p_try = p * dec
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                s = float(p_try.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                p_try = p_try / s
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_try)):
                    continue
                e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                if not np.isfinite(e) or not np.isfinite(s2):
                    continue
                if s2 + 1e-11 * max(1.0, ch_smooth) < ch_smooth:
                    chosen = p_try
                    ch_smooth = s2
                    ch_exact = e
                    ch_a = a

            if chosen is not None:
                p = chosen
                cur_smooth = ch_smooth
                cur_exact = ch_exact
                alpha = ch_a
                stall = 0

                if cur_exact < best_exact:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_exact = cur_exact
                    if verbose and (it - last_print >= 250):
                        last_print = it
                        print(f"mmd n={m} iter={it} best={best_exact:.6f}")

                # If the working point drifted badly, pull it back.
                if cur_exact > best_exact * 1.25 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-4, alpha * 0.6)

                if stall >= 12:
                    std = 0.05 if stall < 40 else min(0.4, 0.05 + 0.005 * stall)
                    p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, m)))
                    if p_try is not None:
                        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                        p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                        e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                        if np.isfinite(e) and e <= best_exact * 1.05:
                            p = p_try
                            cur_exact, cur_smooth = e, s2
                        else:
                            p = best.copy()
                            cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
                    stall = 0

                if stall > 80:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
                    alpha = 0.1
                    beta = max(beta, float(beta_start))
                    stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 20 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.15)

    return best, best_exact


def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit):
    """Exact coordinate search: multiply random contiguous blocks."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, _exact_score(p)

    floor = 1e-18 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(p)
    cur_score = best_score
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stagnant = 0

    factors = (0.35, 0.65, 0.85, 1.15, 1.35, 1.75, 2.5)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.30:
            w = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 200 + 1)))
        elif r < 0.75:
            w = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 200), max(3, n // 40 + 1)))
        else:
            low = max(3, n // 40)
            high = max(low + 1, min(n, n // 5 + 1))
            w = int(rng.integers(low, high + 1))

        w = max(1, min(n, w))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        base = p.copy()
        current_best = base
        current_score = cur_score

        for f in factors:
            cand = base.copy()
            cand[start:start + w] *= float(f)
            cand = _sanitize_vec(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
            cand = cand / cand.sum()
            sc = _exact_score(cand)
            if sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                current_score = sc
                current_best = cand

        if current_best is not base:
            p = current_best.copy()
            cur_score = current_score
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 60:
                p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)))
                if p_try is not None:
                    p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                    p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                    sc = _exact_score(p_try)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                        p = p_try
                        cur_score = sc
                    else:
                        p = best.copy()
                        cur_score = best_score
                stagnant = 0

        if stagnant > 350:
            p = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            stagnant = 0

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search bounded by budget_s seconds.  Returns a list of non-negative
    floats expected to minimize evaluate_sequence.
    """
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        budget = 60.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(60.0, budget - 30.0)

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007) + 1)

    cands = []

    # Optional parent from previous state of the art.
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent_raw = None

    parent_arr = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        try:
            parent_arr = _sanitize_vec(np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=float))
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        _add_candidate(cands, parent_arr, _exact_score(parent_arr), 12)

    # ---- Short exploration on a modest length to find a good basin ----
    small_n = 512
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 90.0:
        small_starts = []

        tb = _two_block_best(small_n)
        if tb is not None:
            tb = _smooth_array(tb, 0.5)
            if tb is not None:
                small_starts.append(tb)

        if parent_arr is not None:
            sp = _resample(parent_arr, small_n)
            if sp is not None:
                sp = _smooth_array(sp, 0.5)
            if sp is not None:
                small_starts.append(sp)

        for mode in (1, 2, 4, 0):
            small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, mode))

        for p0 in small_starts:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 25.0:
                break
            talloc = min(30.0, max(5.0, rem - 10.0))
            p, sc = _run_mmd(p0, rng, talloc, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=500.0, verbose=False)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 8)

        _prune_candidates(cands, 8)

    # Work at a moderate length.  This is large enough for good behavior
    # while keeping FFT cost low.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 700.0:
        final_n = 3072
    else:
        final_n = 2048

    # ---- Build diverse final-length starts ----
    final_start_list = []

    tb = _two_block_best(final_n)
    if tb is not None:
        tb = _smooth_array(tb, 1.0)
        if tb is not None:
            final_start_list.append(tb)

    if parent_arr is not None:
        sp = _resample(parent_arr, final_n)
        if sp is not None:
            sp = _smooth_array(sp, 0.75)
        if sp is not None:
            sp = _sanitize_vec(sp * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, final_n)))
        if sp is not None:
            final_start_list.append(sp)

    sorted_cands = sorted(cands, key=_cand_score_key)
    added = 0
    for item in sorted_cands:
        if added >= 2:
            break
        arr = item[2]
        if arr is None:
            continue
        sp = _resample(arr, final_n)
        if sp is not None:
            sp = _sanitize_vec(sp * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, final_n)))
            final_start_list.append(sp)
            added += 1

    for mode in (1, 2, 3, 4, 0):
        final_start_list.append(_initial_sequence(final_n, rng, mode))

    score_starts = []
    for sp in final_start_list:
        if sp is None:
            continue
        sc = _exact_score(sp)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            score_starts.append((sc, sp))

    score_starts.sort(key=_scored_key)

    if not score_starts:
        sp = _initial_sequence(final_n, rng, 1)
        score_starts = [(_exact_score(sp), sp)]

    starts = [sp for sc, sp in score_starts[:4]]
    if not starts:
        starts = [_initial_sequence(final_n, rng, 1)]

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # ---- Main sequential runs, best start gets the largest chunk ----
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 120.0:
        # The search is already short; just return the best available start.
        if score_starts:
            best_arr = score_starts[0][1]
            best_score = score_starts[0][0]
    else:
        reserve = max(240.0, min(600.0, 0.25 * rem)) if rem > 400.0 else 60.0
        main_budget = max(60.0, rem - reserve)
        fracs = [0.40, 0.25, 0.20, 0.15]

        for idx, sp in enumerate(starts):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 80.0:
                break
            frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.10
            talloc = main_budget * frac
            talloc = min(talloc, max(30.0, rem - max(60.0, reserve * 0.1)))
            if talloc < 20.0:
                talloc = min(max(0.0, rem - 30.0), 30.0)

            p, sc = _run_mmd(
                sp,
                rng,
                max(10.0, talloc),
                beta_start=30.0,
                beta_end=1500.0,
                verbose=True,
            )
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 12)

            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_arr = p.copy()
                best_score = sc

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if best_score < 1.52 and rem > 260.0:
                break

    # If nothing was found, fall back to the best start.
    if best_arr is None:
        if score_starts:
            best_arr = score_starts[0][1]
            best_score = score_starts[0][0]
        else:
            best_arr = _initial_sequence(final_n, rng, 1)
            best_score = _exact_score(best_arr)

    best_arr = _sanitize_vec(best_arr)
    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = np.ones(final_n, dtype=float)

    # ---- Polish the best sequence at current length ----
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        t1 = min(0.5 * rem, 600.0)
        p, sc = _run_mmd(
            best_arr,
            rng,
            t1,
            beta_start=80.0,
            beta_end=2500.0,
            verbose=True,
        )
        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 12)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_arr = p.copy()
            best_score = sc

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        p, sc = _local_block_search(best_arr, rng, min(0.5 * rem, 400.0))
        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 12)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_arr = p.copy()
            best_score = sc

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        p, sc = _run_mmd(
            best_arr,
            rng,
            min(0.5 * rem, 250.0),
            beta_start=150.0,
            beta_end=3000.0,
            verbose=True,
        )
        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 12)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_arr = p.copy()
            best_score = sc

    # ---- Optional up-polish to a larger length ----
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and best_arr is not None:
        bign = max(4096, 2 * int(best_arr.size))
        if bign <= 12288:
            big = _resample(best_arr, bign)
            if big is not None:
                t2 = min(90.0, 0.35 * rem)
                pb, scb = _run_mmd(
                    big,
                    rng,
                    t2,
                    beta_start=100.0,
                    beta_end=3000.0,
                    verbose=True,
                )
                _add_candidate(cands, pb, scb, 12)
                if pb is not None and np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_score:
                    best_arr = pb.copy()
                    best_score = scb

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 60.0:
                    pl, scl = _local_block_search(
                        pb if pb is not None else big,
                        rng,
                        min(40.0, 0.5 * rem),
                    )
                    _add_candidate(cands, pl, scl, 12)
                    if pl is not None and np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
                        best_arr = pl.copy()
                        best_score = scl

    # ---- Final exact checks with the official evaluator if available ----
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = _exact_score(best_arr)

    final_cands = [best_arr]
    sorted_cands = sorted(cands, key=_cand_score_key)
    for item in sorted_cands[:5]:
        if item[2] is not None:
            final_cands.append(item[2])
    if parent_arr is not None:
        final_cands.append(parent_arr)

    selected_list = [float(x) for x in best_arr]
    evaluated = 0

    for arr in final_cands:
        a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
        if a is None:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 4.0:
            break
        if evaluated >= 4:
            break
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            sc = _exact_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            selected_list = lst
        evaluated += 1

    out = np.asarray(selected_list, dtype=float)
    if out.size == 0:
        out = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=float)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(max(1, final_n), dtype=float)
    else:
        out = out / s

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
